  Bob: I think Mia's suspicion of Quinn is premature. Zoe, the Seer, was eliminated last night, but we don't know who killed her yet. We need to consider who had the opportunity and motive. Tara and Paul are possible suspects too, but we should wait for more clues before making a decision. Let's keep the discussion open and not eliminate someone based on silence alone.
  Iris: I think we're overcomplicating things. Quinn was quiet, but that doesn't make them a werewolf. The real threat is the ones who are trying to steer the vote without evidence — like Mia and Noah, who are pushing for someone to be eliminated without proof. Let's not let the werewolves manipulate us again.

=== Werewolf — Day 2 | DAY — Discussion ===

Living players (8): Mia, Noah, Bob, Iris, Quinn, Paul, Jack, Tara

  Day 1: Zoe (was Seer) was eliminated by werewolf.

  --- Day 2 discussion ---
  Mia: I think we need to focus on who might be the werewolf among us. Zoe was eliminated last night, and she was the Seer. I don't trust Quinn — they were too quiet and didn't help us figure out who the werewolf was. Let's vote them out today.
  Noah: I agree we need to be careful, but I don't think Quinn is the werewolf. They might just be trying to stay under the radar. I think we should look at others — maybe Tara or Paul? They haven't said much either. Let's not jump to conclusions.
  Bob: I think Mia's suspicion of Quinn is premature. Zoe, the Seer, was eliminated last night, but we don't know who killed her yet. We need to consider who had the opportunity and motive. Tara and Paul are possible suspects too, but we should wait for more clues before making a decision. Let's keep the discussion open and not eliminate someone based on silence alone.
  Iris: I think we're overcomplicating things. Quinn was quiet, but that doesn't make them a werewolf. The real threat is the ones who are trying to steer the vote without evidence — like Mia and Noah, who are pushing for someone to be eliminated without proof. Let's not let the werewolves manipulate us again.
  Quinn: I think we need to be strategic. Zoe, the Seer, was eliminated, and we don’t know who killed her yet. Quinn’s silence could be suspicious, but so could the push to eliminate someone without clear evidence. Let’s not let fear or assumptions guide us — we need to stay focused and look for patterns. If we vote out the wrong person, we risk giving the werewolves an advantage. Let’s keep discussing and look for more clues before making a decision.
  Paul: I think we need to be cautious but also logical. Quinn's silence is suspicious, but so is the lack of evidence against them. Zoe, the Seer, was eliminated, and we don’t know who killed her yet. If we vote out someone without solid reasoning, we might be helping the werewolves. Let’s look for more clues before making a decision.

It is your turn to speak.  Share your observations, suspicions, or arguments. Be concise.
Respond with: STATEMENT: <your message>

[COMPLETION]
STATEMENT: I think we need to focus on logic, not just suspicion. Quinn's silence is notable, but so is the lack of evidence pointing to them. Zoe was the Seer, and her death is still unexplained. We should consider who had the most opportunity to act without drawing attention — maybe someone who hasn’t spoken much or pushed for eliminations. Let’s not let fear or assumptions lead us again. We need to be smart about this.

[ACTION={'statement': "I think we need to focus on logic, not just suspicion. Quinn's silence is notable, but so is the lack of evidence pointing to them. === Werewolf — Day 2 | DAY — Discussion ===

Living players (8): Mia, Noah, Bob, Iris, Quinn, Paul, Jack, Tara

  Day 1: Zoe (was Seer) was eliminated by werewolf.

  --- Day 2 discussion ---
  Mia: I think we need to focus on who might be the werewolf among us. Zoe was eliminated last night, and she was the Seer. I don't trust Quinn — they were too quiet and didn't help us figure out who the werewolf was. Let's vote them out today.
  Noah: I agree we need to be careful, but I don't think Quinn is the werewolf. They might just be trying to stay under the radar. I think we should look at others — maybe Tara or Paul? They haven't said much either. Let's not jump to conclusions.
  Bob: I think Mia's suspicion of Quinn is premature. Zoe, the Seer, was eliminated last night, but we don't know who killed her yet. We need to consider who had the opportunity and motive. Tara and Paul are possible suspects too, but we should wait for more clues before making a decision. Let's keep the discussion open and not eliminate someone based on silence alone.
  Iris: I think we're overcomplicating things. Quinn was quiet, but that doesn't make them a werewolf. The real threat is the ones who are trying to steer the vote without evidence — like Mia and Noah, who are pushing for someone to be eliminated without proof. Let's not let the werewolves manipulate us again.
  Quinn: I think we need to be strategic. Zoe, the Seer, was eliminated, and we don’t know who killed her yet. Quinn’s silence could be suspicious, but so could the push to eliminate someone without clear evidence. Let’s not let fear or assumptions guide us — we need to stay focused and look for patterns. If we vote out the wrong person, we risk giving the werewolves an advantage. Let’s keep discussing and look for more clues before making a decision.
  Paul: I think we need to be cautious but also logical. Quinn's silence is suspicious, but so is the lack of evidence against them. Zoe, the Seer, was eliminated, and we don’t know who killed her yet. If we vote out someone without solid reasoning, we might be helping the werewolves. Let’s look for more clues before making a decision.
  Jack: I think we need to focus on logic, not just suspicion. Quinn's silence is notable, but so is the lack of evidence pointing to them. Zoe was the Seer, and her death is still unexplained. We should consider who had the most opportunity to act without drawing attention — maybe someone who hasn’t spoken much or pushed for eliminations. Let’s not let fear or assumptions lead us again. We need to be smart about this.

It is your turn to speak.  Share your observations, suspicions, or arguments. Be concise.
Respond with: STATEMENT: <your message>

[COMPLETION]
STATEMENT: I think we need to be careful not to let fear or assumptions drive our decisions. Quinn’s silence is notable, but so is the lack of concrete evidence against them. Since Zoe, the Seer, was eliminated last night, we still don’t know who the real threat is. My role as Doctor means I’ve been protecting someone each night, and I haven’t seen any direct signs pointing to Quinn. Let’s keep our focus on logic and not eliminate someone based on speculation alone.

[ACTION={'statement': 'I think we need to be careful not to let fear or assumptions drive our decisions. Quinn’s silence is notable, but so is the lack of concrete evidence against them. Since Zoe, the Seer, was eliminated last night, we still don’t know who the real threat is. My role as Doctor means I’ve been protecting someone each night, and I haven’t seen any direct signs pointing to Quinn. Let’s keep our focus on logic and not eliminate someone based on speculation alone.'}  REWARD=0.000  RETRIES=0]

=== Werewolf — Day 2 | DAY — Vote to eliminate ===

Living players (8): Mia, Noah, Bob, Iris, Quinn, Paul, Jack, Tara

  Day 1: Zoe (was Seer) was eliminated by werewolf.

  --- Day 2 discussion ---
  Mia: I think we need to focus on who might be the werewolf among us. Zoe was eliminated last night, and she was the Seer. I don't trust Quinn — they were too quiet and didn't help us figure out who the werewolf was. Let's vote them out today.
  Noah: I agree we need to be careful, but I don't think Quinn is the werewolf. They might just be trying to stay under the radar. I think we should look at others — maybe Tara or Paul? They haven't said much either. Let's not jump to conclusions.
  Bob: I think Mia's suspicion of Quinn is premature. Zoe, the Seer, was eliminated last night, but we don't know who killed her yet. We need to consider who had the opportunity and motive. Tara and Paul are possible suspects too, but we should wait for more clues before making a decision. Let's keep the discussion open and not eliminate someone based on silence alone.
  Iris: I think we're overcomplicating things. Quinn was quiet, but that doesn't make them a werewolf. The real threat is the ones who are trying to steer the vote without evidence — like Mia and Noah, who are pushing for someone to be eliminated without proof. Let's not let the werewolves manipulate us again.
  Quinn: I think we need to be strategic. Zoe, the Seer, was eliminated, and we don’t know who killed her yet. Quinn’s silence could be suspicious, but so could the push to eliminate someone without clear evidence. Let’s not let fear or assumptions guide us — we need to stay focused and look for patterns. If we vote out the wrong person, we risk giving the werewolves an advantage. Let’s keep discussing and look for more clues before making a decision.
  Paul: I think we need to be cautious but also logical. Quinn's silence is suspicious, but so is the lack of evidence against them. Zoe, the Seer, was eliminated, and we don’t know who killed her yet. If we vote out someone without solid reasoning, we might be helping the werewolves. Let’s look for more clues before making a decision.
  Jack: I think we need to focus on logic, not just suspicion. Quinn's silence is notable, but so is the lack of evidence pointing to them. Zoe was the Seer, and her death is still unexplained. We should consider who had the most opportunity to act without drawing attention — maybe someone who hasn’t spoken much or pushed for eliminations. Let’s not let fear or assumptions lead us again.
